Mnemonic: *What's in fashion is whatever **you hang** in your closet this season.*
Picture a closet rail where everyone is hanging the same trendy jacket. Both syllables are covered by one natural English phrase, and the image (clothes on hangers) points straight at the core meaning of 유행: fashion, trend, or vogue.

Sentence Meaning: Street cats are pitiful.

Mnemonic: **"A *bull sang* a sad song."**
Picture a lonely, skinny **bull** standing in the rain who **sang** (*ssang*) a heartbreaking song, then let out a tired "**ha**..." You can't help thinking, "Aww, poor thing!" That's 불쌍하다.
